# v26s results: sizing COMBO26 in one account with CenterPoint's commissions

Pre-registered in [V26S_SIZING_PREREGISTRATION.md](V26S_SIZING_PREREGISTRATION.md); run 39737844000 (2026-10-22), 48 jobs, all passed.

## Summary

- **The owner's rule picks risk 1% a trade at every account and both commissions** (cap 40-60%, gross 1-2x). At $100k and $0.003 a share: Sortino 5.48, 93% a year on the starting capital (135% compounded, overstated), max DD -52.7%, worst day -27.6%, chance of a 75% drawdown in ten years 0.1%. With $0.006 a share: 85% a year, DD -54%.
- **More size is not allowed by the rule**: risk 1.5% -> chance of a 75% drawdown 6-9% (DD -70%, worst day -41%); 2% -> 38-45% (DD -82%); 3% and more -> 66-95%.
- **Every book is ruinous at the high costs** (spreads x2, borrow x3): at 1% risk about 0% a year, DD -92% to -95%, chance of a 75% drawdown 76-88%.
- CenterPoint's locate fees are not modelled.
